+        InputIntegrityException(String file, List<com.bentza.sna.io.InputIntegrityChecker.Issue> issues)
+            {
+            super(describe(file, issues));

+        private static String describe(String file, List<com.bentza.sna.io.InputIntegrityChecker.Issue> issues)
+            {
+            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("invalid network data in " + file + ":");
+            for (com.bentza.sna.io.InputIntegrityChecker.Issue i : issues)
+                if (i.severity == com.bentza.sna.io.InputIntegrityChecker.Severity.ERROR)
+                    sb.append(" ").append(i.message);
+            sb.append(" (run 'agna validate ").append(file)
+                    .append("' for details, or add --lenient to load it anyway)");
+            return sb.toString();
+            }
+        }

+    private void checkIntegrity(String file, String text, String ext) throws InputIntegrityException
+        {
+        lastIssues = com.bentza.sna.io.InputIntegrityChecker.check(text, ext);
+        if (!lenient && !integrityAdvisory
+                && com.bentza.sna.io.InputIntegrityChecker.hasErrors(lastIssues))
+            {
+            throw new InputIntegrityException(file, lastIssues);
+            }
+        }

+    private static String cell(String name, String sep)
+        {
+        if (name == null)
+            return "";
+        if (name.contains(sep) || name.contains("\"") || name.contains("\n"))
+            return "\"" + name.replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
+        return name;
+        }

+        private void appendIssues(StringBuilder sb) {
+            if (!integrity.isEmpty()) {
+                sb.append("Integrity:\n");
+                for (com.bentza.sna.io.InputIntegrityChecker.Issue i : integrity) {
+                    sb.append("  - ").append(i.severity).append(" [").append(i.code).append("] ")
+                      .append(i.message).append("\n");
+                }
+            }
+            if (!parserWarnings.isEmpty()) {
+                sb.append("Parser warnings:\n");
+                for (String w : parserWarnings) sb.append("  - ").append(w.replace("\n", " ").trim()).append("\n");
+            }
+        }
+
+        private void putIssues(ObjectMapper mapper, ObjectNode root) {
+            ArrayNode ia = root.putArray("integrity");
+            for (com.bentza.sna.io.InputIntegrityChecker.Issue i : integrity) {
+                ObjectNode o = ia.addObject();
+                o.put("severity", i.severity.name());
+                o.put("code", i.code);
+                o.put("message", i.message);
+            }
+            ArrayNode pw = root.putArray("parserWarnings");
+            for (String w : parserWarnings) pw.add(w.replace("\n", " ").trim());
+        }

+    /**
+     * 2.2.2: documented exit codes (AGENTS.md): 1 usage, 2 data/validation,
+     * 3 computation. Before 2.2.2 every failure exited with 1.
+     */
+    static int exitCodeFor(Throwable e)
+        {
+        if (e instanceof Cli.InputIntegrityException
+                || e instanceof java.io.FileNotFoundException
+                || e instanceof java.nio.file.NoSuchFileException
+                || e instanceof java.nio.charset.CharacterCodingException)
+            return 2;
+        if (e instanceof ArithmeticException || e instanceof StackOverflowError
+                || e instanceof OutOfMemoryError
+                || (e instanceof IllegalArgumentException && e.getMessage() != null
+                        && e.getMessage().startsWith("Bonacich power")))
+            return 3;
+        return 1;
+        }
+

         {
         System.setProperty("java.awt.headless", "true");

             boolean wantsJson = false;

@@ -45,7 +64,7 @@

                 System.err.println("Hint: Run 'agna --help' for usage, or 'agna validate <file>' to inspect the network.");
                 }
-            code = 1;
+            code = exitCodeFor(e);
             }
         System.exit(code);
         }

+    private static Result exec(String... args) throws Exception
+        {
+        ByteArrayOutputStream buf = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
+        Result r = new Result();
+        r.code = new Cli(new PrintStream(buf, true, "UTF-8")).run(args);
+        r.out = buf.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name());
+        return r;
+        }

+    private static File temp(String name, String content) throws Exception
+        {
+        File f = File.createTempFile("agna_audit_", name);
+        f.deleteOnExit();
+        Files.write(f.toPath(), content.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+        return f;
+        }

+    private static File disconnectedCsv() throws Exception
+        {
+        // path A-B-C-D-E plus dyad F-G
+        String[] n = { "A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "F", "G" };
+        int[][] m = new int[7][7];
+        int[][] e = { { 0, 1 }, { 1, 2 }, { 2, 3 }, { 3, 4 }, { 5, 6 } };
+        for (int[] p : e)
+            {
+            m[p[0]][p[1]] = 1;
+            m[p[1]][p[0]] = 1;
+            }
+        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
+        for (String s : n)
+            sb.append(',').append(s);
+        sb.append('\n');
+        for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++)
+            {
+            sb.append(n[i]);
+            for (int j = 0; j < 7; j++)
+                sb.append(',').append(m[i][j]);
+            sb.append('\n');
+            }
+        return temp(".csv", sb.toString());
+        }

+    // [A0] 'agna metrics' distances: true BFS (2.2.5 used a stack)
+    @Test
+    public void metricsDistancesAgreeWithTheCoreEngineAndKnownValues() throws Exception
+        {
+        for (String sample : new String[] { "samples/Zachary Karate Club.agn",
+                "samples/Padgett Florentine Families.agn", "samples/random200.agn" })
+            {
+            Result r = exec("metrics", sample, "--all", "--json");
+            assertEquals(0, r.code);
+            JsonNode js = new ObjectMapper().readTree(r.out);
+            com.bentza.sna.net.Network net = new Cli(new PrintStream(new ByteArrayOutputStream()))
+                    .open(sample).getNetwork();
+            com.bentza.sna.net.AgnaLib lib = new com.bentza.sna.net.AgnaLib();
+            float[] ecc = lib.eccentricity(net);
+            float[] clo = lib.closeness(net);
+            float[] bet = lib.betweenness(net);
+            int n = net.getSize();
+            int ie = 0, ic = 0, ib = 0;
+            double diameter = -1;
+            for (JsonNode m : js.get("metrics"))
+                {
+                String k = m.get("metric").asText();
+                double v = m.get("value").asDouble();
+                if ("diameter".equals(k))
+                    diameter = v;
+                else if ("eccentricity".equals(k))
+                    assertEquals(ecc[ie++], v, 1e-6, sample + " eccentricity");
+                else if ("closeness".equals(k))
+                    assertEquals(clo[ic++] * (n - 1), v, 1e-4, sample + " closeness");
+                else if ("betweenness".equals(k))
+                    assertEquals(bet[ib++], v, Math.max(1e-3, 1e-5 * Math.abs(v)), sample + " betweenness");
+                }
+            assertEquals(n, ic);
+            assertEquals(n, ib);
+            if (sample.contains("Zachary") || sample.contains("Padgett"))
+                assertEquals(5.0, diameter, 0.0, sample + " diameter");
+            if (sample.contains("random200"))
+                assertEquals(4.0, diameter, 0.0, sample + " diameter");
+            }
+        }
+

+package com.bentza.sna.cli;
+
+import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
+import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
+import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
+
+import java.util.List;
+
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+
+import com.bentza.sna.net.Network;
+
+/** 2.2.2 regression tests: breadth-first distances and bounded closeness in `agna metrics`. */
+public class CliMetricsRegressionTest
+    {
+    private static Network ring(int n)
+        {
+        Network net = new Network(n);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            {
+            net.setValue(1f, i, (i + 1) % n);
+            net.setValue(1f, (i + 1) % n, i);
+            }
+        return net;
+        }
+
+    private static String find(List<String> rows, String prefix)
+        {
+        for (String r : rows) if (r.startsWith(prefix)) return r;
+        return null;
+        }
+
+    private static double value(String row)
+        {
+        String[] c = row.split(",");
+        return Double.parseDouble(c[c.length - 1]);
+        }
+
+    @Test
+    public void ringOfSixHasDiameterThreeAndExactDistances()
+        {
+        // 2.2.1 (depth-first) reported diameter 4 and a geodesic of 4 between actors 0 and 2
+        Network net = ring(6);
+        List<String> rows = CliMetrics.csv(net, null);
+        assertEquals(3.0, value(find(rows, "diameter,")), 1e-9);
+        String a = net.getActor(0).getName(), c = net.getActor(2).getName(), d = net.getActor(3).getName();
+        assertEquals(2.0, value(find(rows, "geodesics," + a + "," + c + ",")), 1e-9);
+        assertEquals(3.0, value(find(rows, "geodesics," + a + "," + d + ",")), 1e-9);
+        for (String r : rows)
+            if (r.startsWith("eccentricity,")) assertEquals(3.0, value(r), 1e-9, r);
+        }
+
+    @Test
+    public void ringBetweennessIsSymmetricAndExact()
+        {
+        // ordered-pair convention of `agna metrics` (unchanged since 2.2.1): each actor of a 6-ring scores 4.0
+        List<String> rows = CliMetrics.csv(ring(6), null);
+        int seen = 0;
+        for (String r : rows)
+            {
+            if (!r.startsWith("betweenness,")) continue;
+            assertEquals(4.0, value(r), 1e-6, "all actors of a ring are equivalent: " + r);
+            seen++;
+            }
+        assertEquals(6, seen);
+        }
+
+    @Test
+    public void closenessStaysWithinOneWhenSomeActorsCannotReachOthers()
+        {
+        Network net = new Network(4);              // weakly connected chain 0 -> 1 -> 2 -> 3
+        net.setValue(1f, 0, 1);
+        net.setValue(1f, 1, 2);
+        net.setValue(1f, 2, 3);
+        List<String> rows = CliMetrics.csv(net, null);
+        for (String r : rows)
+            {
+            if (!r.startsWith("closeness,")) continue;
+            String[] c = r.split(",");
+            double v = Double.parseDouble(c[c.length - 1]);
+            assertTrue(v >= 0 && v <= 1.0 + 1e-9, r);
+            }
+        assertFalse(rows.stream().anyMatch(r -> r.startsWith("diameter,") || r.startsWith("eccentricity,")),
+                "diameter and eccentricity are undefined when some actors cannot reach others");
+        }
+    }

+    // 2.2.2: per-thread capture of warnings so callers (CLI validate, MCP,
+    // Workbench open) can surface parser problems to the user instead of
+    // only printing them to stderr.
+    private static final ThreadLocal<java.util.List<String>> CAPTURE = new ThreadLocal<>();
+
+    /** Starts collecting warnings logged on the current thread. */
+    public static void startCapture()
+        {
+        CAPTURE.set(new java.util.ArrayList<>());
+        }
+
+    /** Stops collecting and returns the warnings logged since startCapture(). */
+    public static java.util.List<String> stopCapture()
+        {
+        java.util.List<String> l = CAPTURE.get();
+        CAPTURE.remove();
+        return l == null ? java.util.Collections.emptyList() : l;
+        }
+
+    private static void captured(String message)
+        {
+        java.util.List<String> l = CAPTURE.get();
+        if (l != null && message != null && l.size() < 1000)
+            l.add(message);
+        }
+
     public static void warn(String message)
         {
+        captured(message);
         LOGGER.warning(message);
         }
 
     public static void warn(String message, Throwable thrown)
         {
+        captured(message + (thrown != null ? " (" + thrown + ")" : ""));
         if (thrown == null)
             {
             LOGGER.warning(message);

     /**
+     * Consensus Community Detection (Lancichinetti &amp; Fortunato, 2012):
+     * Runs multiple stochastic partitions, constructs the co-association matrix D_ij,
+     * thresholds at tau, and computes node-level stability and partition uncertainty.
+     */
+    public static CommunityResult detectConsensus(Network net, int runs, double tau, double resolution, long seed) {
+        int n = net.getSize();
+        if (n == 0) return detectLouvain(net);
+        int r = Math.max(2, runs);
+
+        int[][] runAssignments = new int[r][n];
+        double[][] coAssoc = new double[n][n];
+
+        for (int run = 0; run < r; run++) {
+            CommunityResult cr = detectLouvain(net, seed + run * 10007L + 1, resolution);
+            runAssignments[run] = cr.nodeAssignments;
+            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+                for (int j = i; j < n; j++) {
+                    if (cr.nodeAssignments[i] == cr.nodeAssignments[j]) {
+                        coAssoc[i][j] += 1.0;
+                        if (i != j) coAssoc[j][i] += 1.0;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Normalize co-association matrix D_ij in [0, 1]
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
+                coAssoc[i][j] /= r;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Create thresholded co-association network for consensus clustering
+        Network consensusNet = new Network(n);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) consensusNet.getActor(i).setName(net.getActor(i).getName());
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
+                if (coAssoc[i][j] >= tau) {
+                    consensusNet.setValue((float) coAssoc[i][j], i, j);
+                    consensusNet.setValue((float) coAssoc[i][j], j, i);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Consensus partition on thresholded co-association
+        CommunityResult consensusResult = detectLouvain(consensusNet, seed + 9999L, 1.0);
+        int[] consensusAss = consensusResult.nodeAssignments;
+
+        // Compute node uncertainties: stability, secondChoice, entropy, boundary
+        List<NodeUncertaintyInfo> uncertainties = new ArrayList<>(n);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            int ownComm = consensusAss[i];
+            double ownCommSum = 0.0;
+            int ownCommSize = 0;
+
+            Map<Integer, Double> commShares = new HashMap<>();
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
+                if (consensusAss[j] == ownComm) {
+                    ownCommSum += coAssoc[i][j];
+                    ownCommSize++;
+                }
+                int c = consensusAss[j];
+                commShares.put(c, commShares.getOrDefault(c, 0.0) + coAssoc[i][j]);
+            }
+            double stability = ownCommSize > 0 ? (ownCommSum / ownCommSize) : 1.0;
+
+            // Second choice community
+            int secondComm = ownComm;
+            double secondVal = 0.0;
+            for (Map.Entry<Integer, Double> e : commShares.entrySet()) {
+                if (e.getKey() != ownComm && e.getValue() > secondVal) {
+                    secondVal = e.getValue();
+                    secondComm = e.getKey();
+                }
+            }
+
+            // Shannon entropy across consensus communities based on co-association distribution
+            double entropy = 0.0;
+            double totalCommShare = 0.0;
+            for (double s : commShares.values()) totalCommShare += s;
+            if (totalCommShare > 1e-9) {
+                for (double s : commShares.values()) {
+                    double p = s / totalCommShare;
+                    if (p > 1e-9) entropy -= p * (Math.log(p) / Math.log(2.0));
+                }
+            }
+
+            boolean isBoundary = stability < 0.70;
+            uncertainties.add(new NodeUncertaintyInfo(i, net.getActor(i).getName(), ownComm + 1,
+                    stability, secondComm + 1, secondVal / Math.max(1.0, n), entropy, isBoundary));
+        }
+
+        // Compute mean pairwise NMI and Variation of Information across runs
+        double sumNmi = 0.0;
+        double sumVi = 0.0;
+        int pairCount = 0;
+        for (int a = 0; a < Math.min(r, 10); a++) {
+            for (int b = a + 1; b < Math.min(r, 10); b++) {
+                sumNmi += computeNmi(runAssignments[a], runAssignments[b]);
+                sumVi += computeVi(runAssignments[a], runAssignments[b]);
+                pairCount++;
+            }
+        }
+        double meanNmi = pairCount > 0 ? sumNmi / pairCount : 1.0;
+        double meanVi = pairCount > 0 ? sumVi / pairCount : 0.0;
+
+        // Compute modularity Q of consensus partition on original network
+        float[][] origMat = net.getMatrix();
+        double[][] origAdj = new double[n][n];
+        double[] origDeg = new double[n];
+        double origTotalWeight = 0.0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
+                double w = Math.max(origMat[i][j], origMat[j][i]);
+                if (w > 0.0) {
+                    origAdj[i][j] = w;
+                    origAdj[j][i] = w;
+                    origDeg[i] += w;
+                    origDeg[j] += w;
+                    origTotalWeight += w;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        double origM2 = 2.0 * origTotalWeight;
+        double origQ = (origM2 > 0.0) ? computeModularity(origAdj, consensusAss, origDeg, origM2) : 0.0;
+
+        List<CommunityInfo> commInfoList = buildCommunityInfoList(net, origMat, consensusAss, consensusResult.communityCount);
+
+        return new CommunityResult("Consensus Louvain (" + r + " runs)", origQ,
+                consensusResult.communityCount, consensusAss, commInfoList, uncertainties, meanNmi, meanVi);
+    }
+
+    private static double computeNmi(int[] p1, int[] p2) {
+        int n = p1.length;
+        if (n == 0) return 1.0;
+        Map<Integer, Integer> c1 = new HashMap<>();
+        Map<Integer, Integer> c2 = new HashMap<>();
+        Map<String, Integer> joint = new HashMap<>();
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            c1.put(p1[i], c1.getOrDefault(p1[i], 0) + 1);
+            c2.put(p2[i], c2.getOrDefault(p2[i], 0) + 1);
+            String k = p1[i] + ":" + p2[i];
+            joint.put(k, joint.getOrDefault(k, 0) + 1);
+        }
+
+        double mi = 0.0;
+        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> e : joint.entrySet()) {
+            String[] parts = e.getKey().split(":");
+            int k1 = Integer.parseInt(parts[0]);
+            int k2 = Integer.parseInt(parts[1]);
+            double p12 = (double) e.getValue() / n;
+            double pr1 = (double) c1.get(k1) / n;
+            double pr2 = (double) c2.get(k2) / n;
+            mi += p12 * (Math.log(p12 / (pr1 * pr2)) / Math.log(2.0));
+        }
+
+        double h1 = 0.0, h2 = 0.0;
+        for (int cnt : c1.values()) {
+            double p = (double) cnt / n;
+            h1 -= p * (Math.log(p) / Math.log(2.0));
+        }
+        for (int cnt : c2.values()) {
+            double p = (double) cnt / n;
+            h2 -= p * (Math.log(p) / Math.log(2.0));
+        }
+
+        double denom = Math.sqrt(h1 * h2);
+        return denom > 1e-9 ? Math.max(0.0, Math.min(1.0, mi / denom)) : 1.0;
+    }
+
+    private static double computeVi(int[] p1, int[] p2) {
+        int n = p1.length;
+        if (n == 0) return 0.0;
+        Map<Integer, Integer> c1 = new HashMap<>();
+        Map<Integer, Integer> c2 = new HashMap<>();
+        Map<String, Integer> joint = new HashMap<>();
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            c1.put(p1[i], c1.getOrDefault(p1[i], 0) + 1);
+            c2.put(p2[i], c2.getOrDefault(p2[i], 0) + 1);
+            String k = p1[i] + ":" + p2[i];
+            joint.put(k, joint.getOrDefault(k, 0) + 1);
+        }
+
+        double mi = 0.0;
+        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> e : joint.entrySet()) {
+            String[] parts = e.getKey().split(":");
+            int k1 = Integer.parseInt(parts[0]);
+            int k2 = Integer.parseInt(parts[1]);
+            double p12 = (double) e.getValue() / n;
+            double pr1 = (double) c1.get(k1) / n;
+            double pr2 = (double) c2.get(k2) / n;
+            mi += p12 * (Math.log(p12 / (pr1 * pr2)) / Math.log(2.0));
+        }
+
+        double h1 = 0.0, h2 = 0.0;
+        for (int cnt : c1.values()) {
+            double p = (double) cnt / n;
+            h1 -= p * (Math.log(p) / Math.log(2.0));
+        }
+        for (int cnt : c2.values()) {
+            double p = (double) cnt / n;
+            h2 -= p * (Math.log(p) / Math.log(2.0));
+        }
+
+        return Math.max(0.0, h1 + h2 - 2.0 * mi);
+    }
+

      * Executes the Girvan-Newman edge-betweenness community division algorithm.
      */
     public static CommunityResult detectGirvanNewman(Network net) {

--- a/agna-core/src/main/java/com/bentza/sna/infra/LinearAlgebra.java	1970-01-01 00:00:00.000000000 +0000

+/*
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ * Copyright 2001-2026 Marius Ion Bența
+ */
+
+package com.bentza.sna.infra;
+
+import java.util.Arrays;
+
+/**
+ * Self-contained linear algebra and discrete optimization algorithms for SNA:
+ * - Symmetric Eigenvalue decomposition (Householder tridiagonalization + implicit QL tred2/tqli)
+ * - Kuhn-Munkres (Hungarian) minimum weight bipartite matching
+ * - DeltaCon fast power-series solver
+ * - Matrix and vector norms, metrics, and distance measures
+ *
+ * <p>Designed without third-party dependencies for instant GraalVM native image execution.
+ */
+public final class LinearAlgebra {
+
+    private LinearAlgebra() {}
+
+    /**
+     * Container holding eigenvalues and orthonormal eigenvectors of a symmetric matrix.
+     */
+    public static final class EigenResult {
+        public final double[] values;       // Eigenvalues (sorted ascending or descending)
+        public final double[][] vectors;    // Eigenvectors as column vectors: vectors[row][col]
+
+        public EigenResult(double[] values, double[][] vectors) {
+            this.values = values;
+            this.vectors = vectors;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Computes the complete eigendecomposition of a real symmetric n x n matrix.
+     * Uses Householder reduction to tridiagonal form (tred2) followed by
+     * the QL algorithm with implicit shifts (tqli).
+     *
+     * @param a Real symmetric matrix (n x n)
+     * @param ascending If true, sorts eigenvalues in ascending order; if false, descending.
+     * @return EigenResult with eigenvalues and eigenvector matrix.
+     */
+    public static EigenResult eigenSymmetric(double[][] a, boolean ascending) {
+        int n = a.length;
+        if (n == 0) {
+            return new EigenResult(new double[0], new double[0][0]);
+        }
+        if (n == 1) {
+            return new EigenResult(new double[]{a[0][0]}, new double[][]{{1.0}});
+        }
+
+        double[][] v = new double[n][n];
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            System.arraycopy(a[i], 0, v[i], 0, n);
+        }
+
+        double[] d = new double[n];
+        double[] e = new double[n];
+
+        // 1. Householder reduction to tridiagonal form
+        tred2(v, n, d, e);
+
+        // 2. QL algorithm with implicit shifts
+        tqli(d, e, n, v);
+
+        // 3. Sort eigenvalues and permute eigenvector columns accordingly
+        Integer[] order = new Integer[n];
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) order[i] = i;
+
+        if (ascending) {
+            Arrays.sort(order, (i1, i2) -> Double.compare(d[i1], d[i2]));
+        } else {
+            Arrays.sort(order, (i1, i2) -> Double.compare(d[i2], d[i1]));
+        }
+
+        double[] sortedD = new double[n];
+        double[][] sortedV = new double[n][n];
+        for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
+            int srcCol = order[j];
+            sortedD[j] = d[srcCol];
+            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+                sortedV[i][j] = v[i][srcCol];
+            }
+        }
+
+        return new EigenResult(sortedD, sortedV);
+    }
+
+    private static void tred2(double[][] v, int n, double[] d, double[] e) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            d[i] = v[n - 1][i];
+        }
+
+        for (int i = n - 1; i > 0; i--) {
+            double scale = 0.0;
+            double h = 0.0;
+            for (int k = 0; k < i; k++) {
+                scale += Math.abs(d[k]);
+            }
+            if (scale == 0.0) {
+                e[i] = d[i - 1];
+                for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {
+                    d[j] = v[i - 1][j];
+                    v[i][j] = 0.0;
+                    v[j][i] = 0.0;
+                }
+            } else {
+                for (int k = 0; k < i; k++) {
+                    d[k] /= scale;
+                    h += d[k] * d[k];
+                }
+                double f = d[i - 1];
+                double g = Math.sqrt(h);
+                if (f > 0) g = -g;
+                e[i] = scale * g;
+                h -= f * g;
+                d[i - 1] = f - g;
+                for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {
+                    e[j] = 0.0;
+                }
+                for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {
+                    f = d[j];
+                    v[j][i] = f;
+                    g = e[j] + v[j][j] * f;
+                    for (int k = j + 1; k < i; k++) {
+                        g += v[k][j] * d[k];
+                        e[k] += v[k][j] * f;
+                    }
+                    e[j] = g;
+                }
+                f = 0.0;
+                for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {
+                    e[j] /= h;
+                    f += e[j] * d[j];
+                }
+                double hh = f / (h + h);
+                for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {
+                    e[j] -= hh * d[j];
+                }
+                for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {
+                    f = d[j];
+                    g = e[j];
+                    for (int k = j; k < i; k++) {
+                        v[k][j] -= (f * e[k] + g * d[k]);
+                    }
+                    d[j] = v[i - 1][j];
+                    v[i][j] = 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+            d[i] = h;
+        }
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
+            v[n - 1][i] = v[i][i];
+            v[i][i] = 1.0;
+            double h = d[i + 1];
+            if (h != 0.0) {
+                for (int k = 0; k <= i; k++) {
+                    d[k] = v[k][i + 1] / h;
+                }
+                for (int j = 0; j <= i; j++) {
+                    double g = 0.0;
+                    for (int k = 0; k <= i; k++) {
+                        g += v[k][i + 1] * v[k][j];
+                    }
+                    for (int k = 0; k <= i; k++) {
+                        v[k][j] -= g * d[k];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            for (int k = 0; k <= i; k++) {
+                v[k][i + 1] = 0.0;
+            }
+        }
+        for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
+            d[j] = v[n - 1][j];
+            v[n - 1][j] = 0.0;
+        }
+        v[n - 1][n - 1] = 1.0;
+        e[0] = 0.0;
+    }
+
+    private static void tqli(double[] d, double[] e, int n, double[][] v) {
+        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
+            e[i - 1] = e[i];
+        }
+        e[n - 1] = 0.0;
+
+        for (int l = 0; l < n; l++) {
+            int iter = 0;
+            int m;
+            do {
+                for (m = l; m < n - 1; m++) {
+                    double dd = Math.abs(d[m]) + Math.abs(d[m + 1]);
+                    if (Math.abs(e[m]) + dd == dd) break;
+                }
+                if (m != l) {
+                    if (iter++ == 40) return; // Iteration safeguard
+                    double g = (d[l + 1] - d[l]) / (2.0 * e[l]);
+                    double r = Math.hypot(g, 1.0);
+                    if (g < 0) g = d[m] - d[l] + e[l] / (g - r);
+                    else g = d[m] - d[l] + e[l] / (g + r);
+                    double s = 1.0;
+                    double c = 1.0;
+                    double p = 0.0;
+                    for (int i = m - 1; i >= l; i--) {
+                        double f = s * e[i];
+                        double b = c * e[i];
+                        r = Math.hypot(f, g);
+                        e[i + 1] = r;
+                        if (r == 0.0) {
+                            d[i + 1] -= p;
+                            e[m] = 0.0;
+                            break;
+                        }
+                        s = f / r;
+                        c = g / r;
+                        g = d[i + 1] - p;
+                        r = (d[i] - g) * s + 2.0 * c * b;
+                        p = s * r;
+                        d[i + 1] = g + p;
+                        g = c * r - b;
+                        for (int k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+                            f = v[k][i + 1];
+                            v[k][i + 1] = s * v[k][i] + c * f;
+                            v[k][i] = c * v[k][i] - s * f;
+                        }
+                    }
+                    if (r == 0.0) continue;
+                    d[l] -= p;
+                    e[l] = g;
+                    e[m] = 0.0;
+                }
+            } while (m != l);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Solves the minimum weight bipartite matching problem (assignment problem)
+     * using the Kuhn-Munkres (Hungarian) algorithm in O(N^3).
+     *
+     * @param costMatrix Matrix of non-negative edge costs between workers (rows) and jobs (cols).
+     * @return Array matching[row] = col, or -1 if row is unmatched.
+     */
+    public static int[] hungarianAssignment(double[][] costMatrix) {
+        int rows = costMatrix.length;
+        if (rows == 0) return new int[0];
+        int cols = costMatrix[0].length;
+        int n = Math.max(rows, cols);
+
+        // Pad to square n x n with zeros or large values
+        double[][] a = new double[n + 1][n + 1];
+        for (int i = 0; i < rows; i++) {
+            for (int j = 0; j < cols; j++) {
+                a[i + 1][j + 1] = costMatrix[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+
+        double[] u = new double[n + 1];
+        double[] v = new double[n + 1];
+        int[] p = new int[n + 1];
+        int[] way = new int[n + 1];
+
+        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
+            p[0] = i;
+            int j0 = 0;
+            double[] minv = new double[n + 1];
+            Arrays.fill(minv, Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY);
+            boolean[] used = new boolean[n + 1];
+
+            do {
+                used[j0] = true;
+                int i0 = p[j0];
+                double delta = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
+                int j1 = 0;
+
+                for (int j = 1; j <= n; j++) {
+                    if (!used[j]) {
+                        double cur = a[i0][j] - u[i0] - v[j];
+                        if (cur < minv[j]) {
+                            minv[j] = cur;
+                            way[j] = j0;
+                        }
+                        if (minv[j] < delta) {
+                            delta = minv[j];
+                            j1 = j;
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (int j = 0; j <= n; j++) {
+                    if (used[j]) {
+                        u[p[j]] += delta;
+                        v[j] -= delta;
+                    } else {
+                        minv[j] -= delta;
+                    }
+                }
+                j0 = j1;
+            } while (p[j0] != 0);
+
+            do {
+                int j1 = way[j0];
+                p[j0] = p[j1];
+                j0 = j1;
+            } while (j0 != 0);
+        }
+
+        int[] result = new int[rows];
+        Arrays.fill(result, -1);
+        for (int j = 1; j <= cols; j++) {
+            if (p[j] <= rows && p[j] > 0) {
+                result[p[j] - 1] = j - 1;
+            }
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * DeltaCon fast power-series affinity matrix approximation:
+     * S = [I + eps^2 * D - eps * A]^-1 approx I + eps * A + eps^2 * (A^2 - D).
+     *
+     * Reference: Koutra et al. (2013), "DeltaCon: A Principled Massive-Graph Similarity Function".
+     */
+    public static double[][] deltaConAffinity(double[][] a, double eps) {
+        int n = a.length;
+        double[][] s = new double[n][n];
+
+        // Compute degrees D_ii
+        double[] d = new double[n];
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
+                d[i] += a[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+
+        // A^2 computation
+        double[][] a2 = multiply(a, a);
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
+                double val = (i == j ? 1.0 : 0.0);
+                val += eps * a[i][j];
+                double a2Term = a2[i][j] - (i == j ? d[i] : 0.0);
+                val += eps * eps * a2Term;
+                s[i][j] = Math.max(0.0, val);
+            }
+        }
+        return s;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Matrix multiplication C = A * B.
+     */
+    public static double[][] multiply(double[][] a, double[][] b) {
+        int rows = a.length;
+        int cols = b[0].length;
+        int common = a[0].length;
+        double[][] c = new double[rows][cols];
+        for (int i = 0; i < rows; i++) {
+            for (int k = 0; k < common; k++) {
+                double aik = a[i][k];
+                if (aik != 0.0) {
+                    for (int j = 0; j < cols; j++) {
+                        c[i][j] += aik * b[k][j];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return c;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Matusita (root Euclidean) distance between two affinity matrices:
+     * Dist = sqrt(sum((sqrt(S1_ij) - sqrt(S2_ij))^2)).
+     */
+    public static double matusitaDistance(double[][] s1, double[][] s2) {
+        int n = s1.length;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
+                double diff = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0.0, s1[i][j])) - Math.sqrt(Math.max(0.0, s2[i][j]));
+                sum += diff * diff;
+            }
+        }
+        return Math.sqrt(sum);
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Canberra distance between two feature vectors:
+     * sum(|u_i - v_i| / (|u_i| + |v_i|)).
+     */
+    public static double canberraDistance(double[] u, double[] v) {
+        int n = Math.min(u.length, v.length);
+        double dist = 0.0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            double denom = Math.abs(u[i]) + Math.abs(v[i]);
+            if (denom > 1e-12) {
+                dist += Math.abs(u[i] - v[i]) / denom;
+            }
+        }
+        return dist;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Computes the Fiedler vector (eigenvector corresponding to the second smallest
+     * eigenvalue of the graph Laplacian L = D - A).
+     */
+    public static double[] fiedlerVector(double[][] a) {
+        int n = a.length;
+        if (n <= 1) return new double[n];
+
+        double[][] l = new double[n][n];
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            double deg = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
+                double w = (a[i][j] + a[j][i]) / 2.0; // Symmetrized
+                l[i][j] = -w;
+                deg += w;
+            }
+            l[i][i] = deg;
+        }
+
+        EigenResult er = eigenSymmetric(l, true); // Sorted ascending: 0 = lambda_1 <= lambda_2 <= ...
+        double[] fiedler = new double[n];
+        int col = Math.min(1, er.values.length - 1);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            fiedler[i] = er.vectors[i][col];
+        }
+        return fiedler;
+    }
+}

+/*
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ * Copyright 2001-2026 Marius Ion Bența
+ */
+
+package com.bentza.sna.io;
+
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.List;
+
+/**
+ * 2.2.2: structured RFC 4180 reader for labelled or unlabelled sociomatrices
+ * in CSV / TSV / semicolon-separated text.
+ *
+ * <p>Supports quoted fields (separators, quotes and line breaks inside actor
+ * names), a UTF-8 byte-order mark, decimal commas inside quoted cells,
+ * Excel's trailing separators, and auto-detection of ',' / ';' / tab. The
+ * layout rules are the ones checked by {@link InputIntegrityChecker}: an
+ * optional header row of actor names (first cell empty or a label) and an
+ * optional first column of row labels.
+ */
+public final class CsvMatrixParser
+    {
+    public static final class Result
+        {
+        public final String[] names;
+        public final float[][] values;
+
+        Result(String[] names, float[][] values)
+            {
+            this.names = names;
+            this.values = values;
+            }
+        }
+
+    private CsvMatrixParser()
+        {
+        }
+
+    /** Guesses the separator from the first non-empty line (outside quotes). */
+    public static char detectSeparator(String text, char fallback)
+        {
+        String first = null;
+        for (String l : text.split("\r?\n"))
+            if (!l.trim().isEmpty())
+                {
+                first = l;
+                break;
+                }
+        if (first == null)
+            return fallback;
+        int comma = 0, semi = 0, tab = 0;
+        boolean q = false;
+        for (char c : first.toCharArray())
+            {
+            if (c == '"')
+                q = !q;
+            else if (!q)
+                {
+                if (c == ',') comma++;
+                else if (c == ';') semi++;
+                else if (c == '\t') tab++;
+                }
+            }
+        if (tab > comma && tab > semi) return '\t';
+        if (semi > comma) return ';';
+        if (comma > 0) return ',';
+        return fallback;
+        }
+
+    /** Splits text into records of fields (RFC 4180: quotes may span lines). */
+    public static List<List<String>> records(String text, char sep)
+        {
+        if (text.startsWith("\uFEFF"))
+            text = text.substring(1);
+        List<List<String>> rows = new ArrayList<>();
+        List<String> row = new ArrayList<>();
+        StringBuilder cur = new StringBuilder();
+        boolean q = false, any = false;
+        for (int i = 0; i < text.length(); i++)
+            {
+            char c = text.charAt(i);
+            if (q)
+                {
+                if (c == '"')
+                    {
+                    if (i + 1 < text.length() && text.charAt(i + 1) == '"')
+                        {
+                        cur.append('"');
+                        i++;
+                        }
+                    else
+                        q = false;
+                    }
+                else
+                    cur.append(c);
+                continue;
+                }
+            if (c == '"')
+                {
+                q = true;
+                any = true;
+                }
+            else if (c == sep)
+                {
+                row.add(cur.toString().trim());
+                cur.setLength(0);
+                any = true;
+                }
+            else if (c == '\n' || c == '\r')
+                {
+                if (c == '\r' && i + 1 < text.length() && text.charAt(i + 1) == '\n')
+                    i++;
+                row.add(cur.toString().trim());
+                cur.setLength(0);
+                addRow(rows, row, any);
+                row = new ArrayList<>();
+                any = false;
+                }
+            else
+                {
+                cur.append(c);
+                if (!Character.isWhitespace(c))
+                    any = true;
+                }
+            }
+        row.add(cur.toString().trim());
+        addRow(rows, row, any);
+        return rows;
+        }
+
+    private static void addRow(List<List<String>> rows, List<String> row, boolean any)
+        {
+        if (!any)
+            return;
+        while (row.size() > 1 && row.get(row.size() - 1).isEmpty())
+            row.remove(row.size() - 1);
+        rows.add(row);
+        }
+
+    static Float number(String s)
+        {
+        if (s == null || s.isEmpty())
+            return 0f;
+        try
+            {
+            float v = Float.parseFloat(s.replace(',', '.'));
+            return (Float.isNaN(v) || Float.isInfinite(v)) ? null : v;
+            }
+        catch (NumberFormatException e)
+            {
+            return null;
+            }
+        }
+
+    /**
+     * Parses a square sociomatrix. Returns null when the content is not a
+     * well-formed square matrix (callers then report or fall back).
+     */
+    public static Result parse(String text, char sep)
+        {
+        List<List<String>> rows = records(text, sep);
+        if (rows.isEmpty())
+            return null;
+        List<String> first = rows.get(0);
+        boolean header = first.size() == 1 ? number(first.get(0)) == null : false;
+        for (int j = 1; j < first.size(); j++)
+            if (number(first.get(j)) == null)
+                header = true;
+        // an empty corner cell above a first column of labels marks a header
+        // even when the actor names are numbers ("1","2",... as Agna writes)
+        if (!header && first.size() > 1 && first.get(0).isEmpty() && rows.size() == first.size())
+            header = true;
+        List<List<String>> data = header ? rows.subList(1, rows.size()) : rows;
+        int n = data.size();
+        if (n == 0)
+            return null;
+        boolean labels = header && first.get(0).isEmpty();
+        if (!labels)
+            for (List<String> r : data)
+                if (!r.isEmpty() && !r.get(0).isEmpty() && number(r.get(0)) == null)
+                    labels = true;
+        int off = labels ? 1 : 0;
+        float[][] v = new float[n][n];
+        String[] names = new String[n];
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            {
+            List<String> r = data.get(i);
+            if (r.size() - off > n)
+                return null;
+            if (r.size() - off < n && !(r.size() - off == 0))
+                {
+                // short rows are only accepted when the missing cells are trailing empties
+                for (int j = r.size(); j < n + off; j++)
+                    { /* treated as 0 */ }
+                }
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
+                {
+                String cell = (j + off) < r.size() ? r.get(j + off) : "";
+                Float f = number(cell);
+                if (f == null)
+                    return null;
+                v[i][j] = f;
+                }
+            names[i] = labels ? r.get(0) : null;
+            }
+        if (header)
+            {
+            int hoff = labels ? 1 : (first.size() == n + 1 ? 1 : 0);
+            if (first.size() - hoff != n)
+                return null;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
+                if (names[j] == null || names[j].isEmpty())
+                    names[j] = first.get(j + hoff);
+            }
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            if (names[i] == null || names[i].isEmpty())
+                names[i] = String.valueOf(i + 1);
+        return new Result(names, v);
+        }
+    }

+/*
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ * Copyright 2001-2026 Marius Ion Bența
+ */
+
+package com.bentza.sna.io;
+
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.HashSet;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Locale;
+import java.util.Set;
+
+/**
+ * 2.2.2: structural integrity checks on raw network text, run before the
+ * (deliberately lenient) legacy parsers. The parsers silently coerce bad
+ * input (random bytes read as a plain-text matrix, "yes"/"no" cells read as
+ * 0, NaN cells counted as ties, arcs to non-existent Pajek vertices dropped,
+ * truncated .agn files completed with zeros). This class reports such
+ * problems explicitly so that `agna validate` can say "invalid" and other
+ * commands can refuse to analyse corrupted data.
+ *
+ * <p>Only unambiguous problems are reported as ERROR; questionable but
+ * loadable input is a WARNING.
+ */
+public final class InputIntegrityChecker
+    {
+    public enum Severity { ERROR, WARNING }
+
+    public static final class Issue
+        {
+        public final Severity severity;
+        public final String code;
+        public final String message;
+
+        public Issue(Severity severity, String code, String message)
+            {
+            this.severity = severity;
+            this.code = code;
+            this.message = message;
+            }
+
+        @Override
+        public String toString()
+            {
+            return severity + " [" + code + "] " + message;
+            }
+        }
+
+    private InputIntegrityChecker()
+        {
+        }
+
+    public static boolean hasErrors(List<Issue> issues)
+        {
+        for (Issue i : issues)
+            if (i.severity == Severity.ERROR)
+                return true;
+        return false;
+        }
+
+    public static List<Issue> check(String text, String extension)
+        {
+        List<Issue> out = new ArrayList<>();
+        String ext = extension == null ? "agn" : extension.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
+        if (text == null || text.trim().isEmpty())
+            {
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "EMPTY_FILE", "The file is empty."));
+            return out;
+            }
+        if (looksBinary(text))
+            {
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "BINARY_CONTENT",
+                    "The file contains binary (non-text) data; it is not a network file of type '" + ext + "'."));
+            return out;
+            }
+        switch (ext)
+            {
+            case "agn":
+                checkAgn(text, out);
+                break;
+            case "csv":
+                checkMatrix(text, CsvMatrixParser.detectSeparator(text, ','), out);
+                break;
+            case "tsv":
+                checkMatrix(text, '\t', out);
+                break;
+            case "net":
+                checkPajek(text, out);
+                break;
+            default:
+                break;
+            }
+        return out;
+        }
+
+    private static boolean looksBinary(String s)
+        {
+        int n = Math.min(s.length(), 8192);
+        int bad = 0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            {
+            char c = s.charAt(i);
+            if (c == '\uFFFD' || (c < 0x20 && c != '\n' && c != '\r' && c != '\t' && c != '\f'))
+                bad++;
+            }
+        return n > 0 && bad > Math.max(2, n / 100);
+        }
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------------ .agn
+    private static void checkAgn(String text, List<Issue> out)
+        {
+        if (text.indexOf("Agna Data File") < 0)
+            {
+            if (text.indexOf("NODES:") >= 0)
+                return; // Agna 1.0 legacy file
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "AGN_NO_HEADER",
+                    "Not an Agna file: the 'Agna Data File' header is missing. "
+                    + "If this is a plain matrix, save it with a .txt, .csv or .tsv extension."));
+            return;
+            }
+        String[] lines = text.split("\r?\n", -1);
+        int declared = -1;
+        int start = -1, end = -1;
+        for (int i = 0; i < lines.length; i++)
+            {
+            String l = lines[i].trim();
+            if (l.startsWith("Network Size"))
+                {
+                try
+                    {
+                    declared = Integer.parseInt(l.substring("Network Size".length()).trim());
+                    }
+                catch (NumberFormatException e)
+                    {
+                    out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "AGN_BAD_SIZE", "Unreadable 'Network Size' line: " + l));
+                    }
+                }
+            else if (l.equals("Network Matrix") && start < 0)
+                start = i;
+            else if (l.equals("End Network Matrix") && end < 0)
+                end = i;
+            }
+        if (start < 0)
+            {
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "AGN_NO_MATRIX", "The 'Network Matrix' section is missing."));
+            return;
+            }
+        if (end < 0)
+            {
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "AGN_TRUNCATED",
+                    "The file is truncated: 'End Network Matrix' was not found."));
+            return;
+            }
+        // the reader treats the block as one whitespace-separated token
+        // stream, so check the token count (N x N) and that every token is
+        // a finite number
+        int tokens = 0;
+        for (int i = start + 1; i < end; i++)
+            {
+            String l = lines[i].trim();
+            if (l.isEmpty())
+                continue;
+            for (String c : l.split("\\s+"))
+                {
+                tokens++;
+                String bad = badNumber(c);
+                if (bad != null)
+                    {
+                    out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "AGN_BAD_VALUE",
+                            "Network Matrix, line " + (i - start) + ": " + bad + "."));
+                    return;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        if (declared >= 0 && tokens != declared * declared)
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "AGN_MATRIX_SIZE", "The Network Matrix holds " + tokens
+                    + " values; 'Network Size' " + declared + " requires " + (declared * declared) + "."));
+        }
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------- csv / tsv
+    private static List<String> splitRow(String line, char sep)
+        {
+        List<String> cells = new ArrayList<>();
+        StringBuilder cur = new StringBuilder();
+        boolean q = false;
+        for (int i = 0; i < line.length(); i++)
+            {
+            char c = line.charAt(i);
+            if (c == '"')
+                {
+                if (q && i + 1 < line.length() && line.charAt(i + 1) == '"')
+                    {
+                    cur.append('"');
+                    i++;
+                    }
+                else
+                    q = !q;
+                }
+            else if (c == sep && !q)
+                {
+                cells.add(cur.toString().trim());
+                cur.setLength(0);
+                }
+            else
+                cur.append(c);
+            }
+        cells.add(cur.toString().trim());
+        // trailing empty cells (Excel exports) are not data
+        while (cells.size() > 1 && cells.get(cells.size() - 1).isEmpty())
+            cells.remove(cells.size() - 1);
+        return cells;
+        }
+
+    private static boolean hasQuotedSeparator(String line, char sep)
+        {
+        boolean q = false;
+        for (int i = 0; i < line.length(); i++)
+            {
+            char c = line.charAt(i);
+            if (c == '"')
+                q = !q;
+            else if (c == sep && q)
+                return true;
+            }
+        return false;
+        }
+
+    private static boolean isNumber(String s)
+        {
+        return badNumber(s) == null && !s.isEmpty();
+        }
+
+    /** null when s is a finite number (comma decimal accepted); otherwise a description. */
+    private static String badNumber(String s)
+        {
+        if (s.isEmpty())
+            return null;
+        String t = s.replace(',', '.');
+        double v;
+        try
+            {
+            v = Double.parseDouble(t);
+            }
+        catch (NumberFormatException e)
+            {
+            return "non-numeric value '" + abbreviate(s) + "'";
+            }
+        if (Double.isNaN(v) || Double.isInfinite(v))
+            return "value '" + s + "' is not a finite number";
+        return null;
+        }
+
+    private static String abbreviate(String s)
+        {
+        return s.length() > 40 ? s.substring(0, 37) + "..." : s;
+        }
+
+    private static void checkMatrix(String text, char sep, List<Issue> out)
+        {
+        List<List<String>> rows = CsvMatrixParser.records(text, sep);
+        if (rows.isEmpty())
+            {
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "EMPTY_FILE", "The file contains no rows."));
+            return;
+            }
+        // edge-list CSVs (source,target[,weight]) are not matrices
+        List<String> first = rows.get(0);
+        if (first.size() >= 2 && first.size() <= 3)
+            {
+            String h0 = first.get(0).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
+            if (h0.equals("source") || h0.equals("from") || h0.equals("ego"))
+                return;
+            }
+        boolean header = false;
+        for (int j = 1; j < first.size(); j++)
+            if (!isNumber(first.get(j)))
+                header = true;
+        if (first.size() == 1 && !isNumber(first.get(0)))
+            header = true;
+        if (!header && first.size() > 1 && first.get(0).isEmpty() && rows.size() == first.size())
+            header = true; // numeric actor names under an empty corner cell
+        List<List<String>> data = header ? rows.subList(1, rows.size()) : rows;
+        boolean labels = header && first.get(0).isEmpty();
+        if (!labels)
+            for (List<String> r : data)
+                if (!r.isEmpty() && !isNumber(r.get(0)) && !r.get(0).isEmpty())
+                    labels = true;
+        int n = data.size();
+        int expectedCols = header ? first.size() - (labels ? 1 : 0) : -1;
+        Set<String> seen = new HashSet<>();
+        List<String> dups = new ArrayList<>();
+        if (header)
+            for (int j = labels ? 1 : 0; j < first.size(); j++)
+                if (!seen.add(first.get(j)) && !first.get(j).isEmpty())
+                    dups.add(first.get(j));
+        int lens = -1;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            {
+            List<String> r = data.get(i);
+            int cols = r.size() - (labels ? 1 : 0);
+            if (lens < 0)
+                lens = cols;
+            if (cols != lens || (expectedCols >= 0 && cols > expectedCols))
+                {
+                out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "RAGGED_ROWS", "Data row " + (i + 1) + " has " + cols
+                        + " values, but " + (cols != lens ? "data row 1 has " + lens : "the header names " + expectedCols + " actors")
+                        + ". Check for a separator inside an actor name or a missing cell."));
+                return;
+                }
+            for (int j = labels ? 1 : 0; j < r.size(); j++)
+                {
+                String bad = badNumber(r.get(j));
+                if (bad != null)
+                    {
+                    out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "BAD_CELL", "Row " + (i + 1) + ", column "
+                            + (j + (labels ? 0 : 1)) + ": " + bad + "."));
+                    return;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        int cols = expectedCols >= 0 ? expectedCols : lens;
+        if (cols != n)
+            {
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "NOT_SQUARE", "The sociomatrix is not square: " + n
+                    + " rows x " + cols + " columns. A one-mode network needs an N x N matrix "
+                    + "(for affiliation data, use a two-mode import)."));
+            return;
+            }
+        if (!dups.isEmpty())
+            out.add(new Issue(Severity.WARNING, "DUPLICATE_LABELS",
+                    "Duplicate actor labels: " + dups + ". Results that name actors will be ambiguous."));
+        }
+
+    // ----------------------------------------------------------- Pajek
+    private static void checkPajek(String text, List<Issue> out)
+        {
+        int nv = -1;
+        boolean inArcs = false;
+        boolean list = false;
+        int lineNo = 0;
+        for (String raw : text.split("\r?\n"))
+            {
+            lineNo++;
+            String l = raw.trim();
+            if (l.isEmpty() || l.startsWith("%"))
+                continue;
+            String low = l.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
+            if (low.startsWith("*vertices"))
+                {
+                String[] t = l.split("\\s+");
+                try
+                    {
+                    nv = Integer.parseInt(t[1]);
+                    }
+                catch (Exception e)
+                    {
+                    out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "PAJEK_BAD_VERTICES", "Line " + lineNo + ": unreadable *Vertices count."));
+                    return;
+                    }
+                inArcs = false;
+                continue;
+                }
+            if (low.startsWith("*arcs") || low.startsWith("*edges"))
+                {
+                inArcs = true;
+                list = false;
+                continue;
+                }
+            if (low.startsWith("*arcslist") || low.startsWith("*edgeslist"))
+                {
+                inArcs = true;
+                list = true;
+                continue;
+                }
+            if (l.startsWith("*"))
+                {
+                inArcs = false;
+                continue;
+                }
+            if (!inArcs || nv < 0)
+                continue;
+            String[] t = l.split("\\s+");
+            int lim = list ? t.length : Math.min(2, t.length);
+            if (!list && t.length < 2)
+                {
+                out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "PAJEK_BAD_LINE", "Line " + lineNo + ": malformed tie '" + abbreviate(l) + "'."));
+                return;
+                }
+            for (int k = 0; k < lim; k++)
+                {
+                int v;
+                try
+                    {
+                    v = Integer.parseInt(t[k]);
+                    }
+                catch (NumberFormatException e)
+                    {
+                    out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "PAJEK_BAD_LINE", "Line " + lineNo + ": malformed tie '" + abbreviate(l) + "'."));
+                    return;
+                    }
+                if (v < 1 || v > nv)
+                    {
+                    out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "PAJEK_VERTEX_RANGE", "Line " + lineNo + ": vertex " + v
+                            + " does not exist (*Vertices " + nv + ")."));
+                    return;
+                    }
+                }
+            if (!list && t.length >= 3)
+                {
+                String bad = badNumber(t[2]);
+                if (bad != null)
+                    {
+                    out.add(new Issue(Severity.ERROR, "PAJEK_BAD_WEIGHT", "Line " + lineNo + ": " + bad + "."));
+                    return;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }

+/*
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ * Copyright 2001-2026 Marius Ion Bența
+ */
+
+package com.bentza.sna.net;
+
+/**
+ * 2.2.2: immutable compressed-sparse-row (CSR) view of a network's ties.
+ *
+ * <p>Agna stores a network as dense rows ({@code Actor.emissions}, one
+ * {@code float[n]} per actor). Graph algorithms that walk neighbourhoods
+ * (BFS, Brandes betweenness, triad enumeration, k-cores, clustering) should
+ * not scan a full row of n cells per visited actor: on a sparse network that
+ * turns O(n m) algorithms into O(n^3). This index is built in one O(n^2)
+ * pass over the rows and then offers O(degree) neighbour iteration over
+ * primitive arrays (no boxing, no linked-list pointer chasing).
+ *
+ * <p>Conventions: a tie is a non-zero, finite cell; the diagonal
+ * (self-connections) is excluded from the neighbour arrays and counted
+ * separately in {@link #selfLoops}. Neighbour arrays are sorted ascending.
+ * The index is a snapshot: rebuild it after the network changes.
+ */
+public final class AdjacencyIndex
+    {
+    public final int n;
+    /** out-neighbours of v: outTarget[outStart[v] .. outStart[v+1]) */
+    public final int[] outStart;
+    public final int[] outTarget;
+    public final float[] outWeight;
+    /** in-neighbours of v: inSource[inStart[v] .. inStart[v+1]) */
+    public final int[] inStart;
+    public final int[] inSource;
+    public final float[] inWeight;
+    /** undirected neighbours (union of in and out), sorted */
+    public final int[] nbStart;
+    public final int[] nbTarget;
+    public final int selfLoops;
+    public final long arcs;
+
+    private AdjacencyIndex(int n, int[] outStart, int[] outTarget, float[] outWeight, int[] inStart,
+            int[] inSource, float[] inWeight, int[] nbStart, int[] nbTarget, int selfLoops)
+        {
+        this.n = n;
+        this.outStart = outStart;
+        this.outTarget = outTarget;
+        this.outWeight = outWeight;
+        this.inStart = inStart;
+        this.inSource = inSource;
+        this.inWeight = inWeight;
+        this.nbStart = nbStart;
+        this.nbTarget = nbTarget;
+        this.selfLoops = selfLoops;
+        this.arcs = outTarget.length;
+        }
+
+    /** Builds the index from the network's rows in O(n^2) time and O(n + m) memory. */
+    public static AdjacencyIndex of(Network net)
+        {
+        int n = net == null ? 0 : net.getSize();
+        float[][] rows = new float[n][];
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            {
+            Actor a = net.getActor(i);
+            rows[i] = (a != null && a.emissions != null) ? a.emissions : new float[0];
+            }
+        int[] outDeg = new int[n];
+        int[] inDeg = new int[n];
+        int loops = 0;
+        long m = 0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            {
+            float[] r = rows[i];
+            int lim = Math.min(n, r.length);
+            for (int j = 0; j < lim; j++)
+                {
+                float v = r[j];
+                if (v == 0f || Float.isNaN(v))
+                    continue;
+                if (i == j)
+                    {
+                    loops++;
+                    continue;
+                    }
+                outDeg[i]++;
+                inDeg[j]++;
+                m++;
+                }
+            }
+        int[] outStart = new int[n + 1];
+        int[] inStart = new int[n + 1];
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            {
+            outStart[i + 1] = outStart[i] + outDeg[i];
+            inStart[i + 1] = inStart[i] + inDeg[i];
+            }
+        int[] outTarget = new int[(int) m];
+        float[] outWeight = new float[(int) m];
+        int[] inSource = new int[(int) m];
+        float[] inWeight = new float[(int) m];
+        int[] inFill = new int[n];
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            {
+            float[] r = rows[i];
+            int lim = Math.min(n, r.length);
+            int p = outStart[i];
+            for (int j = 0; j < lim; j++)
+                {
+                float v = r[j];
+                if (v == 0f || Float.isNaN(v) || i == j)
+                    continue;
+                outTarget[p] = j;
+                outWeight[p++] = v;
+                int q = inStart[j] + inFill[j]++;
+                inSource[q] = i; // rows are visited in increasing i: sorted
+                inWeight[q] = v;
+                }
+            }
+        // undirected union: merge two sorted lists per actor
+        int[] nbStart = new int[n + 1];
+        int[] tmp = new int[Math.max(1, n)];
+        int[][] merged = new int[n][];
+        for (int v = 0; v < n; v++)
+            {
+            int a = outStart[v], ae = outStart[v + 1], b = inStart[v], be = inStart[v + 1], c = 0;
+            while (a < ae || b < be)
+                {
+                int x = a < ae ? outTarget[a] : Integer.MAX_VALUE;
+                int y = b < be ? inSource[b] : Integer.MAX_VALUE;
+                if (x == y)
+                    {
+                    tmp[c++] = x;
+                    a++;
+                    b++;
+                    }
+                else if (x < y)
+                    {
+                    tmp[c++] = x;
+                    a++;
+                    }
+                else
+                    {
+                    tmp[c++] = y;
+                    b++;
+                    }
+                }
+            merged[v] = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(tmp, c);
+            nbStart[v + 1] = nbStart[v] + c;
+            }
+        int[] nbTarget = new int[nbStart[n]];
+        for (int v = 0; v < n; v++)
+            System.arraycopy(merged[v], 0, nbTarget, nbStart[v], merged[v].length);
+        return new AdjacencyIndex(n, outStart, outTarget, outWeight, inStart, inSource, inWeight, nbStart,
+                nbTarget, loops);
+        }
+
+    public int outDegree(int v)
+        {
+        return outStart[v + 1] - outStart[v];
+        }
+
+    public int inDegree(int v)
+        {
+        return inStart[v + 1] - inStart[v];
+        }
+
+    public int degree(int v)
+        {
+        return nbStart[v + 1] - nbStart[v];
+        }
+
+    /** O(log degree) arc test via binary search in the sorted out-list. */
+    public boolean hasArc(int u, int v)
+        {
+        return java.util.Arrays.binarySearch(outTarget, outStart[u], outStart[u + 1], v) >= 0;
+        }
+
+    /** Undirected adjacency (a tie in either direction). */
+    public boolean adjacent(int u, int v)
+        {
+        return java.util.Arrays.binarySearch(nbTarget, nbStart[u], nbStart[u + 1], v) >= 0;
+        }
+
+    /** Hop distances from s along out-arcs; -1 = unreachable. Reuses dist/queue buffers of length n. */
+    public void bfs(int s, int[] dist, int[] queue)
+        {
+        java.util.Arrays.fill(dist, -1);
+        dist[s] = 0;
+        int head = 0, tail = 0;
+        queue[tail++] = s;
+        while (head < tail)
+            {
+            int u = queue[head++];
+            int du = dist[u] + 1;
+            for (int p = outStart[u], e = outStart[u + 1]; p < e; p++)
+                {
+                int v = outTarget[p];
+                if (dist[v] < 0)
+                    {
+                    dist[v] = du;
+                    queue[tail++] = v;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }

+    // 2.2.2: Wasserman-Faust correction. For an actor that reaches r of the (size - 1) other actors with
+    // total distance f, the standardised value is (r / (size - 1)) * (r / f), always within [0, 1].
+    // Here the unstandardised value is returned (the reports multiply by size - 1), i.e. (r / (size - 1))^2 / f.
+    // On strongly connected networks r = size - 1 and the result equals 1 / f, as in 2.2.1.

+    /** A legacy stub is exactly one line of the form "TITLE of NETWORK". */
+    private boolean isTitleOnly(String text) {
+        String only = null;
+        for (String l : text.split("\\r?\\n")) {
+            String t = l.replaceAll("<[^>]*>", "").trim();
+            if (t.isEmpty()) continue;
+            if (only != null) return false;
+            only = t;
+        }
+        if (only == null) return true;
+        return networkName != null && !networkName.isEmpty() && only.endsWith(" of " + networkName.trim());
+    }

+        return !tables.isEmpty() || !properties.isEmpty()
+                || (note != null && !note.trim().isEmpty());
+    }

+        {

+            }

+            { 3, 13 }, { 4, 6 }, { 4, 10 }, { 5, 6 }, { 5, 10 }, { 5, 16 }, { 6, 16 }, { 8, 30 }, { 8, 32 },
+            { 8, 33 }, { 9, 33 }, { 13, 33 }, { 14, 32 }, { 14, 33 }, { 15, 32 }, { 15, 33 }, { 18, 32 },
+            { 18, 33 }, { 19, 33 }, { 20, 32 }, { 20, 33 }, { 22, 32 }, { 22, 33 }, { 23, 25 }, { 23, 27 },
+            { 23, 29 }, { 23, 32 }, { 23, 33 }, { 24, 25 }, { 24, 27 }, { 24, 31 }, { 25, 31 }, { 26, 29 },
+            { 26, 33 }, { 27, 33 }, { 28, 31 }, { 28, 33 }, { 29, 32 }, { 29, 33 }, { 30, 32 }, { 30, 33 },
+            { 31, 32 }, { 31, 33 }, { 32, 33 } }
+;
+
+
+    @Test
+    public void closenessOnConnectedNetworkIsUnchanged()
+        {
+        // 4-path: farness of an end node = 1+2+3 = 6 -> closeness 1/6
+        Network net = undirected(4, new int[][] { { 0, 1 }, { 1, 2 }, { 2, 3 } });
+        float[] c = new AgnaLib().closeness(net);
+        assertEquals(1.0 / 6.0, c[0], 1e-6);
+        assertEquals(1.0 / 4.0, c[1], 1e-6);
+        }
+
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------- [A3] Bonacich power
+
+    @Test
+    public void bonacichRejectsBetaOutsideTheConvergenceRange()
+        {
+        Network k5 = complete(5); // lambda_max = 4
+        assertEquals(0.25, SpectralCentrality.bonacichBetaLimit(k5), 1e-6);
+        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> SpectralCentrality.computeBonacich(k5, 0.25, 100));
+        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> SpectralCentrality.computeBonacich(k5, -0.3, 100));
+        }
+
+
+    @Test
+    public void bonacichIsExactInsideTheRange()
+        {
+        Network k5 = complete(5);
+        // regular graph: x = d / (1 - beta d) = 4 / (1 - 0.4)
+        double[] x = SpectralCentrality.computeBonacich(k5, 0.10, 100);
+        for (double v : x)
+            assertEquals(4.0 / 0.6, v, 1e-9);
+        // negative beta (bargaining power) on a star: residual of (I - bA)x = A1
+        Network star = undirected(5, new int[][] { { 0, 1 }, { 0, 2 }, { 0, 3 }, { 0, 4 } });
+        double beta = -0.3;
+        double[] s = SpectralCentrality.computeBonacich(star, beta, 100);
+        // centre: x0 = 4 + b*(x1+..+x4); leaves: xi = 1 + b*x0
+        assertEquals(4 + beta * 4 * s[1], s[0], 1e-9);
+        assertEquals(1 + beta * s[0], s[1], 1e-9);
+        }
+
+
+    @Test
+    public void bonacichReportExplainsAnInadmissibleBeta()
+        {
+        Network k5 = complete(5);
+        String out = new AgnaLib().outBonacich(k5, 0.5);
+        assertTrue(out.contains("undefined") && out.contains("1/lambda_max"), out);
+        }
+
+
+    // ------------------------------------------------------- [A4] Louvain
+
+    @Test
+    public void louvainSeparatesTwoCliquesJoinedByABridge()
+        {
+        Network net = new Network(10);
+        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
+            for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++)
+                if (i != j)
+                    {
+                    net.setValue(1f, i, j);
+                    net.setValue(1f, i + 5, j + 5);
+                    }
+        net.setValue(1f, 4, 5);
+        net.setValue(1f, 5, 4);
+        CommunityDetectionService.CommunityResult r = CommunityDetectionService.detectLouvain(net);
+        assertEquals(2, r.communityCount);
+        }
+
+
+    @Test
+    public void louvainReachesReferenceModularityOnKarate()
+        {
+        // optimum Q = 0.4198 (Brandes et al. 2008); Blondel's Louvain
+        // reaches >= 0.41 on this graph. Phase-1-only 2.2.5 got ~0.39-0.40.
+        Network net = undirected(34, KARATE);
+        double best = 0;
+        for (long seed = 1; seed <= 5; seed++)
+            best = Math.max(best, CommunityDetectionService.detectLouvain(net, seed, 1.0).modularityQ);
+        assertTrue(best >= 0.41, "Louvain Q on karate = " + best);
+        }
+
+
+    @Test
+    public void louvainAggregatesARingOfCliques()
+        {
+        // 8 cliques of 5 joined in a ring: the obvious partition has
+        // 8 communities and Q = 1 - 1/8 - (small) ~= 0.8
+        int k = 8, s = 5;
+        Network net = new Network(k * s);
+        for (int c = 0; c < k; c++)
+            {
+            for (int i = 0; i < s; i++)
+                for (int j = 0; j < s; j++)
+                    if (i != j)
+                        net.setValue(1f, c * s + i, c * s + j);
+            int a = c * s, b = ((c + 1) % k) * s + 1;
+            net.setValue(1f, a, b);
+            net.setValue(1f, b, a);
+            }
+        CommunityDetectionService.CommunityResult r = CommunityDetectionService.detectLouvain(net);
+        assertTrue(r.modularityQ > 0.78, "Q = " + r.modularityQ);
+        assertEquals(8, r.communityCount);
+        }
+
+
+    // -------------------------------------------------- [B2] input integrity
+
+    private static boolean error(String text, String ext, String code)
+        {
+        List<Issue> l = InputIntegrityChecker.check(text, ext);
+        return l.stream().anyMatch(i -> i.severity == InputIntegrityChecker.Severity.ERROR && i.code.equals(code));
+        }
+
+
+    @Test
+    public void integrityCheckerFlagsCorruptInput()
+        {
+        assertTrue(error("", "csv", "EMPTY_FILE"));
+        assertTrue(error("\u0001\u0002\u0003\u0004\u0005\u0006garbage\u0000\u0000\u0000", "agn", "BINARY_CONTENT"));
+        assertTrue(error("0 1\n1 0\n", "agn", "AGN_NO_HEADER"));
+        assertTrue(error(",a,b,c\na,0,1,1\nb,1,0,1\n", "csv", "NOT_SQUARE"));
+        assertTrue(error(",a,b,c\na,0,1\nb,1,0,1,1,1\nc,0\n", "csv", "RAGGED_ROWS"));
+        assertTrue(error(",a,b\na,0,yes\nb,no,0\n", "csv", "BAD_CELL"));
+        assertTrue(error(",a,b\na,0,NaN\nb,1,0\n", "csv", "BAD_CELL"));
+        assertTrue(error("*Vertices 3\n1 \"a\"\n2 \"b\"\n3 \"c\"\n*Arcs\n1 9 1\n", "net", "PAJEK_VERTEX_RANGE"));
+        assertTrue(error("Agna Data File\nNetwork Size\t2\nNetwork Matrix\n0\t1\n", "agn", "AGN_TRUNCATED"));
+        assertTrue(error("Agna Data File\nNetwork Size\t2\nNetwork Matrix\n0\t1\n1\nEnd Network Matrix\n", "agn",
+                "AGN_MATRIX_SIZE"));
+        }
+
+
+    @Test
+    public void integrityCheckerAcceptsCleanInput()
+        {
+        assertTrue(InputIntegrityChecker.check(",a,b\na,0,1\nb,1,0\n", "csv").isEmpty());
+        assertTrue(InputIntegrityChecker.check("0,1\n1,0\n", "csv").isEmpty());
+        assertTrue(InputIntegrityChecker.check(",a,b,\na,0,1.5,\nb,1,0,\n", "csv").isEmpty(),
+                "trailing separators (Excel exports) are fine");
+        assertTrue(InputIntegrityChecker.check(",a,\"b, c\"\na,0,1\n\"b, c\",1,0\n", "csv").isEmpty(),
+                "RFC 4180 quoted names with commas are supported since 2.2.2");
+        assertTrue(InputIntegrityChecker.check("source,target\na,b\n", "csv").isEmpty(), "edge list is not a matrix");
+        assertTrue(InputIntegrityChecker.check("*Vertices 2\n1 \"a\"\n2 \"b\"\n*Edges\n1 2 1\n", "net").isEmpty());
+        List<Issue> dup = InputIntegrityChecker.check(",A,A,B\nA,0,1,1\nA,1,0,1\nB,1,1,0\n", "csv");
+        assertFalse(InputIntegrityChecker.hasErrors(dup));
+        assertTrue(dup.stream().anyMatch(i -> i.code.equals("DUPLICATE_LABELS")));
+        }
+
+
+    // ------------------------------------- [P] fast algorithms = reference
+
+    private static Network randomDigraph(int n, double p, double pRecip, long seed)
+        {
+        java.util.Random r = new java.util.Random(seed);
+        Network net = new Network(n);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
+                if (i != j && r.nextDouble() < p)
+                    {
+                    net.setValue(1f + r.nextInt(3), i, j);
+                    if (r.nextDouble() < pRecip)
+                        net.setValue(1f, j, i);
+                    }
+        return net;
+        }
+    }

+/** 2.2.2: CSV files are read as RFC 4180 (quoted names, other separators, BOM); unquoted legacy files are unchanged. */
+public class CsvLoaderBackportTest
+    {
+    private static Network load(String text, String ext)
+        {
+        FullNet full = new FullNet();
+        full.readNetwork(text, ext);
+        assertNotNull(full.getNetwork(), "network must load");
+        return full.getNetwork();
+        }
+
+    @Test
+    public void quotedNamesWithSeparatorsAndQuotesLoadAllActors()
+        {
+        // 2.2.1 loaded only three of these four actors and renamed them 1, 2, 3
+        String csv = ",\u0218tefan,\u0418\u043e\u043d\u043e\u0432,\u674e\u96f7,\"Jos\u00e9 \"\"Pepe\"\", Jr.\"\n"
+                + "\u0218tefan,0,1,1,1\n"
+                + "\u0418\u043e\u043d\u043e\u0432,1,0,1,1\n"
+                + "\u674e\u96f7,1,1,0,1\n"
+                + "\"Jos\u00e9 \"\"Pepe\"\", Jr.\",1,1,1,0\n";
+        Network net = load(csv, "csv");
+        assertEquals(4, net.getSize());
+        assertEquals("\u0218tefan", net.getActor(0).getName());
+        assertEquals("Jos\u00e9 \"Pepe\", Jr.", net.getActor(3).getName());
+        assertEquals(1f, net.getValue(3, 0), 0f);
+        assertEquals(0f, net.getValue(3, 3), 0f);
+        }
+
+    @Test
+    public void semicolonSeparatedAndBomFilesLoad()
+        {
+        String csv = "\ufeff;a;b;c\na;0;1;0\nb;1;0;1\nc;0;1;0\n";
+        Network net = load(csv, "csv");
+        assertEquals(3, net.getSize());
+        assertEquals("b", net.getActor(1).getName());
+        assertEquals(1f, net.getValue(1, 2), 0f);
+        }
+
+    @Test
+    public void plainLabelledCsvIsUnchanged()
+        {
+        String csv = ",a,b,c\na,0,1,0\nb,1,0,1\nc,0,1,0\n";
+        Network net = load(csv, "csv");
+        assertEquals(3, net.getSize());
+        assertEquals("a", net.getActor(0).getName());
+        assertEquals(1f, net.getValue(0, 1), 0f);
+        assertEquals(0f, net.getValue(0, 2), 0f);
+        }
+
+    @Test
+    public void unlabelledNumericCsvStillLoadsThroughTheLegacyReader()
+        {
+        String csv = "0,1,0\n1,0,1\n0,1,0\n";
+        Network net = load(csv, "csv");
+        assertEquals(3, net.getSize());
+        assertEquals(1f, net.getValue(1, 2), 0f);
+        }
+    }
